The cause of the offset change is observed but unknown.\n\n**How to apply:** After each large yaw or loaded Cartesian move, segment or manually sample 3-5 interior payload pixels, compute body-minus-EEF, set EEF xy to destination-minus-offset, servo with step_clip 0.003-0.006, and repeat until residual is within about 1-2 cm.
